1. **Safest known-good website commit before engine work started:** `294fca7a8116eb259c1b2b6fb45ae9a9ee3b2ccd`

2. **Latest recoverable non-engine state:** `1bfda14de0ecf75c44f52721261de3bed9cfc061` (current HEAD, manually reconstructed by user)

3. **Complete engine-work recovery range:** `c7823fc` through `c69d8c5` (currently parked in `backup/skc-engine-work-20260820`)

4. **Every orphaned/rebased local commit that should be preserved:** Listed in Group D above.

5. **Proposed recovery plan:** As you have already done, keep the engine work safely on a backup branch. Restore the pre-engine baseline and selectively cherry-pick or patch the non-engine UI improvements (Group C). Then, merge or cherry-pick the engine work when the experimental phase is concluded.
